economy_decisions = {
	r56_decision_PRU_nationalize_rubber_development_corporation_subsidiary = {
		priority = 50
		icon = hol_attract_foreign_investors
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
		}
		targets = {
			USA
		}
		allowed = {
			original_tag = PRU
		}
		available = {
			has_idea = PRU_rubber_development_corporation_subsidiary
			num_of_available_civilian_factories > 2
			if = {
				limit = {
					USA = {
						exists = yes
						is_major = yes
					}
				}
				USA = {
					has_opinion = {
						target = ROOT
						value > 49
					}
					NOT = {
						has_war_with = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
		days_remove = 140
		fire_only_once = yes
		visible = {
			has_idea = PRU_rubber_development_corporation_subsidiary
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = PRU_rubber_development_corporation_subsidiary_nationalized
			}
		}
		cost = 25
		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 2
		}
		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = PRU_rubber_development_corporation_subsidiary_nationalized
			add_stability = 0.02
			if = {
				limit = {
					USA = {
						exists = yes
						is_major = yes
					}
				}
				USA = {
					add_opinion_modifier = {
						target = ROOT
						modifier = GEN_disgruntled_investors
					}
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = PRU_rubber_development_corporation_subsidiary
				add_idea = PRU_rubber_development_corporation_subsidiary_nationalized
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = r56_PRU_peruvian_economy_dm_tt
			add_to_variable = {
				local_resources_factor = 0.05
				tooltip = local_resources_factor_tt
			}
		}
	}
	r56_decision_PRU_rural_education_in_FROM = {
		priority = 2
		icon = generic_research
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
		}
		allowed = {
			original_tag = PRU
		}
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		state_target = yes
		target_array = controlled_states
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				OR = {
					has_state_category = pastoral
					has_state_category = rural
				}
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = r56_flag_PRU_rural_education_initiative_finished_here
				}
			}
		}
		available = {
			has_completed_focus = PRU_bring_education_to_the_countryside
			num_of_available_civilian_factories > 1
		}
		days_remove = 50
		fire_only_once = yes
		visible = {
			has_completed_focus = PRU_foundation_for_the_urban_normal_school
			check_variable = {
				r56_PRU_economy_dm_research_speed_factor < 0.15
			}
		}
		cost = 25
		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 1
			political_power_gain = -0.05
		}
		complete_effect = {
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = ROOT
				popularity = 0.02
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = r56_flag_PRU_rural_education_initiative_finished_here
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = r56_PRU_peruvian_economy_dm_tt
			add_to_variable = {
				r56_PRU_economy_dm_research_speed_factor = 0.01
				tooltip = research_speed_factor_tt
			}
		}
	}

PRU_end_radicalism_in_america1 = {
	r56_support_democratic_sentiment_in_FROM = {
		icon = generic_political_rally
		cost = 50
		days_remove = 30
		allowed = {
			original_tag = PRU
		}
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_subject = no
				is_major = no
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = ROOT
				}
				NOT = {
					has_government = democratic
				}
				democratic < 0.5
				capital_scope = {
					is_core_of = FROM
					is_on_continent = south_america
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			consumer_goods_factor = 0.1
			stability_weekly = -0.001
		}
		available = {
			FROM = {
				democratic < 0.2
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			FROM = {
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = democratic
					popularity = 0.05
				}
			}
		}
	}
	r56_end_radicalism_in_FROM = {
		icon = generic_ignite_civil_war
		fire_only_once = yes
		cost = 100
		days_remove = 30
		allowed = {
			original_tag = PRU
		}
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_subject = no
				is_major = no
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = ROOT
				}
				NOT = {
					has_government = democratic
				}
				OR = {
					communism > 0.45
					fascism > 0.45
				}
				capital_scope = {
					is_core_of = FROM
					is_on_continent = south_america
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			FROM = {
				country_event = {
					id = peru.10
					days = 1
					random_days = 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			create_wargoal = {
				type = puppet_wargoal_focus
				target = FROM
				expire = 0
			}
		}
	}

			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = PRU_another_country
			}
		}
		visible = {

		}
		remove_effect = {
			USA = {
				country_event = {
					id = peru.20
					hours = 4
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = PRU_another_country
			}
		}
	}
}

	PRU_settle_command = {

		icon = generic_political_discourse
		cost = 35
		days_remove = 21
		days_re_enable = 90
		available = {
			check_variable = {
				PRU_command_ambition > 24
			}
		
has_country_flag = PRU_command_filled
}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
		}
	
visible = { has_country_flag = PRU_command_filled 
OR = { is_ai = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = PRU_filter_active } has_country_flag = PRU_filter_command }
 }










complete_effect = {
if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_ureta } set_country_flag = PRU_settlement_ureta }
if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_saldias } set_country_flag = PRU_settlement_saldias }
if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_odria } set_country_flag = PRU_settlement_odria }

if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_ramirez } set_country_flag = PRU_settlement_ramirez }
if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_santisteban } set_country_flag = PRU_settlement_santisteban }
if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_montagne_command } set_country_flag = PRU_settlement_montagne_command }
}

cancel_trigger = {
OR = { AND = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_ramirez NOT = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_ramirez } } AND = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_santisteban NOT = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_santisteban } } AND = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_montagne_command NOT = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_montagne_command } } AND = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_ureta NOT = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_ureta } } AND = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_saldias NOT = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_saldias } } AND = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_odria NOT = { has_country_flag = PRU_circle_odria } } }
}

cancel_effect = {
clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_ureta clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_saldias clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_odria

clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_ramirez
clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_santisteban
clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_montagne_command
}

remove_effect = {
if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_ureta has_country_flag = PRU_circle_ureta } add_to_variable = { PRU_trust_ureta = 12 } set_country_flag = { flag = PRU_pact_ureta days = 180 } } clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_ureta
if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_saldias has_country_flag = PRU_circle_saldias } add_to_variable = { PRU_trust_saldias = 12 } set_country_flag = { flag = PRU_pact_saldias days = 180 } } clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_saldias
if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_odria has_country_flag = PRU_circle_odria } add_to_variable = { PRU_trust_odria = 12 } set_country_flag = { flag = PRU_pact_odria days = 180 } } clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_odria PRU_build_consent = yes PRU_caudillismo_refresh = yes

if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_ramirez has_country_flag = PRU_circle_ramirez } add_to_variable = { PRU_trust_ramirez = 12 } set_country_flag = { flag = PRU_pact_ramirez days = 180 } }
if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_santisteban has_country_flag = PRU_circle_santisteban } add_to_variable = { PRU_trust_santisteban = 12 } set_country_flag = { flag = PRU_pact_santisteban days = 180 } }
if = { limit = { has_country_flag = PRU_settlement_montagne_command has_country_flag = PRU_circle_montagne_command } add_to_variable = { PRU_trust_montagne_command = 12 } set_country_flag = { flag = PRU_pact_montagne_command days = 180 } }
clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_ramirez
clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_santisteban
clr_country_flag = PRU_settlement_montagne_command
}
}
	PRU_dismiss_command_decision = {

		icon = generic_political_discourse
		cost = 30
		days_re_enable = 120
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = PRU_command_filled
		
OR = { is_ai = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = PRU_filter_active } has_country_flag = PRU_filter_command }
}
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = PRU_command_appointment_cooldown
			}

			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = PRU_command_filled }
				NOT = { check_variable = { PRU_command_trust < 40 } }
				check_variable = { PRU_command_influence < 45 }
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
		}

complete_effect = {

			PRU_replace_command_consequences = yes
			PRU_clear_command = yes
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = PRU_command_appointment_cooldown
				days = 30
			}

PRU_caudillismo_refresh = yes
}

}

}

			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = PRU_cabinet_appointment_cooldown
			}

				NOT = { has_country_flag = PRU_cabinet_filled }
				NOT = { check_variable = { PRU_cabinet_trust < 40 } }

				days = 30
			}

}

			}

		}

				has_country_flag = PRU_circle_ramirez
			}

				}

					is_in_faction_with = ROOT
				}

							OR = {
								is_on_continent = south_america

							}

				}
			}

								has_government = democratic
							}

							ideology = democratic
							popularity = 0.10
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

			factor = 1
		}
	}
